Search bus and train journeys by route and date, look up cities and stations, and fetch popular routes. Get departure/arrival times, duration, transfers, prices, operators, and amenities. |
| [Trainline Scraper](https://apify.com/crawlerbros/trainline-scraper?fpr=hs6s8) |  | Scrape Trainline - the UK & Europe rail/bus metasearch. Journey search (origin → destination → date/time) with departure/arrival times, duration, changes, operator and fares; station autocomplete lookup; and popular routes with 'from' prices. |
| [Airbnb API Scraper](https://apify.com/rl1987/airbnb-api-scraper?fpr=hs6s8) |  | Scrapes Airbnb listings, details, and reviews via Airbnb's anonymous GraphQL API. |
